Getting started

A few useful first steps.

Create Invoice

Taxes and optional service fees are calculated automatically from Settings.

Add your GST number in Settings before sending invoices.

Invoice details

iInvoice number is generated automatically. Invoice and due dates use your invoice settings.

Number and dates

Customer details

iSelect an existing customer or enter a new customer. New customer details are saved with the invoice.

Billing and contact information

Invoice items *

More invoice options Discount, payment and recurring

Discount

iDiscount is deducted before the service fee and GST are calculated.

Payment

iEnter any amount already paid. The remaining balance due is calculated automatically.

Recurring invoice

iRecurring invoices are generated when the app is opened and a scheduled invoice is due.

Automatic reminders

iThis only affects automatic reminders. The manual reminder button is unchanged.

Part / advance invoice

Optional. Full invoicing remains the default.

For My Accountant

Prepare Frindly records for your accountant or accounting software.

1. Check my accounts

Check your year-end records and accounting accuracy together.

Choose your financial year end and run the check.

Accounting Period

Choose the records to include.

2. Share with my accountant

Download financial reports, transactions, GST, payroll and ledger checks in one ZIP file.

3. Advanced toolsXero export, accounting setup and controlled adjustments

These tools are for accountants and advanced accounting tasks. Posting entries changes your ledger.

Xero Export

Validates account and GST mappings before creating Xero-compatible CSV files.

Historical ledger posting

Creates balanced posted journals for invoices, bills, payments, eligible credits/refunds and equipment depreciation in the selected period. Already posted source records are skipped; refunds that are not safely accounting-backed remain for review.

Ready when chart and mappings are complete

Accounting setup

Manage Frindly accounts, expense categories and optional Xero export mappings.

0% complete
Select a period to check export readiness.

Holiday pay liability

Posts an accounting adjustment only when the required leave-pay evidence is available.

Use Chart of Accounts to manage ledger accounts and Expense Categories to control future expense allocation. Open Advanced only if you need Xero export settings. These controls do not post journals.

Frindly Accounting Setup · Accounts & Expense Categories

Used for Frindly's own accounting reports and future expense postings. Existing posted journals are unchanged when you update a mapping.

Chart of Accounts

These accounts organise your financial reports. Everyday transactions are allocated automatically using your existing mappings. Changes here do not rewrite posted journals.

Invoice display details

These details appear on invoices and quotes. Your main business profile is managed under Business Setup.

Branding & invoice template

Live invoice previewChanges appear here before you save them.

Invoice wording

Change the words and labels that appear on the invoice. The live preview above updates as you type.

Invoice bank payment details

Choose one default account for invoice payment instructions. These accounts also sync into Bank Reconciliation.

Products & default prices

These appear as suggestions while you type a description. You can also enter any custom description. Prices are excluding GST and can still be changed on an individual invoice.

Invoice email wording

These templates are used for invoice emails. Available placeholders: {customerName}, {invoiceNumber}, {tradingName}, {companyName}, {total}, {balanceDue}, {dueDate}, {phone}, {email}.

Payment reminder email wording

Used when sending a payment reminder from an issued invoice. Available placeholders: {customerName}, {invoiceNumber}, {tradingName}, {companyName}, {total}, {amountPaid}, {amountOutstanding}, {overdueAmount}, {dueDate}, {daysOverdue}, {statusLine}, {phone}, {email}.

Automatic payment reminders

Optional. Manual reminders stay available. Automatic reminders only run for issued invoices that have already been emailed and still have an outstanding balance.

Quotation email wording

These templates are used for quotation emails. Available placeholders: {customerName}, {quoteNumber}, {tradingName}, {companyName}, {amountExGst}, {gst}, {total}, {validUntil}, {phone}, {email}.

Subscription Plans

Existing plan management. Plan/module relationships and subscription behaviour are unchanged.

Subscription plans

Create and edit the plans customers can choose. Stripe plans use a Stripe Price ID. Other gateway plan IDs can be added when their checkout adapters are enabled.

Plans & Features Page

Control the customer-facing feature catalogue. Plan prices, limits and module entitlements continue to come from Subscription Plans.

Public feature catalogue

Module-backed rows automatically use each plan's existing included modules. Core rows appear on every public plan. Limit rows use the existing invoice and employee limits.

Modules

Existing Frindly platform module catalogue. Business entitlement logic is unchanged.

Module catalog

Enable a module globally first, then include it in subscription plans or grant it to an individual business. Online Invoice Payments starts disabled and does not grant access to existing businesses until you choose its rollout.

Payroll Rules

Statutory country payroll calculation rules and official-source compliance monitoring. This does not control Payroll module access.

Payroll Compliance

Official source monitoring is separate from production payroll. Detected changes require review, a Draft ruleset, validation, explicit approval and activation.

Loading official-source status…
